# Case Validation Field Logic Catalog

- Status: Generated review catalog
- Scope: Current metadata seed in `source-code/mmria/mmria-server/database-scripts/metadata.json`
- Generated: 2026-05-06
- Scalar fields listed: 1155
- Related docs: [Case Validation Context](./case_validation_context.md), [Case Validation Rule Seed](./case_validation_rule_seed.md), [AI Context Index](./AI_CONTEXT.md)

This catalog is the human-readable review surface for metadata-driven and logical validation. Field ids locate the data, but validation intent is written from the field label, ancestry, nearby fields, and MMRIA workflow meaning. Runtime rules should be generated into the version-scoped `case-validation-rules-{metadata_version}` document and then reviewed by a form designer before broad enforcement.

Validation levels used below: metadata, intrinsic logic, connected logic, timeline logic, conditional logic, and form completeness. V1 findings remain warning-only.
